69→\n    70→%%%----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    71→%%%\n    72→%%% There is a difference between supported and default algorithms. The\n    73→%%% SUPPORTED algorithms can be handled (maybe untested...). The DEFAULT ones\n    74→%%% are announced in ssh_msg_kexinit and in ssh:default_algorithms/0 to the\n    75→%%% user.\n    76→%%%\n    77→%%% A supported algorithm can be requested in the option 'preferred_algorithms',\n    78→%%% but may give unexpected results before being promoted to default.\n    79→%%%\n    80→%%% This makes it possible to add experimental algorithms (in 86→clear_default_algorithms_env() ->\n    87→    application:unset_env(ssh, ?DEFAULT_ALGS).\n    88→\n    89→-spec default_algorithms() -> algs_list()\n    90→                                  | no_return() %  error(Reason)\n    91→                                  .\n    92→default_algorithms() ->\n    93→    FipsMode = crypto:info_fips(),\n    94→    case application:get_env(ssh, ?DEFAULT_ALGS) of\n    95→        undefined ->\n    96→            Algs = build_cache(),\n    97→            application:set_env(ssh, ?DEFAULT_ALGS, {FipsMode,Algs}),\n    98→            Algs;\n    99→\n   100→        {ok,{FipsMode,Algs}} ->\n   101→            %% Cached, and the FIPS mode is the same now as when it was cached.\n   102→            Algs;\n   103→\n   104→        {ok,{_OtherFipsMode,_Algs}} ->\n   105→            %% Cached, but the FIPS mode has changed.\n   106→            Algs = build_cache(),\n   107→            application:set_env(ssh, ?DEFAULT_ALGS, {FipsMode,Algs}),\n   108→            Algs\n   109→    end.\n   110→\n   111→build_cache() ->\n   112→    Opts = get_alg_conf(),\n   113→    Algs1 =\n   114→        case proplists:get_value(preferred_algorithms, Opts) of\n   115→            undefined ->\n   116→                [{K,default_algorithms1(K)} || K <- algo_classes()];\n   117→            Algs0 ->\n   118→                {true,Algs01} = ssh_options:check_preferred_algorithms(Algs0),\n   119→                Algs01\n   120→        end,\n   121→    Algs =\n   122→        case proplists:get_value(modify_algorithms, Opts) of\n   123→            undefined ->\n   124→                Algs1;\n   125→            Modifications ->\n   126→                ssh_options:initial_default_algorithms(Algs1, Modifications)\n   127→        end,\n   128→    Algs.\n   129→\n   130→\n   131→\n   132→get_alg_conf() ->\n   133→    [{T,L} || T <- [preferred_algorithms, modify_algorithms],\n   134→              L <- [application:get_env(ssh, T, [])],\n   135→              L =/= []].\n   136→\n   137→algo_classes() -> [kex, public_key, cipher, mac, compression].\n   138→\n   139→algo_class(kex) -> true;\n   140→algo_class(public_key) -> true;\n   141→algo_class(cipher) -> true;\n   142→algo_class(mac) -> true;\n   143→algo_class(compression) -> true;\n   144→algo_class(_) -> false.\n   145→\n   146→\n   147→algo_two_spec_classes() -> [cipher, mac, compression].\n   148→\n   149→algo_two_spec_class(cipher) -> It's implemented as a `gen_statem` (state machine) in Erlang.\n\n## Key Components\n\n### Directory Structure\n```\nlib/ssh/src/\n├── ssh_connection_handler.erl  # Core FSM, handles connection lifecycle\n├── ssh_transport.erl           # Transport layer (RFC 4253): encryption, MAC\n├── ssh_connection.erl          # Connection protocol (RFC 4254): channels\n├── ssh_message.erl             # Message encoding/decoding\n├── ssh_auth.erl                # Authentication (RFC 4252)\n├── ssh_fsm_userauth_client.erl # Client-side auth FSM\n├── ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl # Server-side auth FSM\n├── ssh.hrl                     # Main header with records\n├── ssh_transport.hrl           # Transport message definitions\n├── ssh_connect.hrl             # Connection message definitions\n└── ssh_auth.hrl                # Auth message definitions\n```\n\n### Protocol Layers\n\n1. **Transport Layer (RFC 4253)** - `ssh_transport.erl`\n   - Protocol version exchange\n   - Key exchange (KEXINIT, NEWKEYS)\n   - Encryption and MAC computation\n   - Service requests\n\n2. **User Authentication (RFC 4252)** - `ssh_auth.erl`, `ssh_fsm_userauth_*.erl`\n   - Authentication methods: publickey, password, keyboard-interactive\n   - User validation\n   - `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST`, `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS/FAILURE`\n\n3. **Connection Protocol (RFC 4254)** - `ssh_connection.erl`\n   - Channel management (open, close, data)\n   - Global requests\n   - Channel requests (exec, shell, subsystem)\n\n## State Machine States\n\nThe `ssh_connection_handler.erl` implements a `gen_statem` with these states:\n\n```erlang\n{wait_for_socket, Role}  % Initial state\n{hello, Role}            % Version string exchange\n{kexinit, Role}          % Key exchange\n{service_request, Role}  % Service negotiation (ssh-userauth)\n{userauth, Role}         % User authentication phase\n{connected, Role}        % Connection established and AUTHENTICATED\n{ext_info, Role, ...}    % Extension info states\n```\n\nWhere `Role` is either `client` or `server`.\n\n## Message Processing Flow\n\n```\nNetwork Socket Input\n  ↓ (info event from tcp)\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, ...)\n  ↓\nssh_transport:handle_packet_part/5 (decrypt & decompress)\n  ↓\nssh_message:decode() (binary to record)\n  ↓\nPattern match on message type\n  ↓\nDispatch to appropriate handler\n```\n\n## Key Records\n\n### SSH Connection State (`#ssh{}` in ssh.hrl)\n\n```erlang\n-record(ssh, {\n    role,                    % client | server\n    algorithms,              % negotiated algorithms\n    session_id,              % session identifier\n    user,                    % authenticated username\n    service,                 % current service\n    authenticated = false,   % CRITICAL: authentication flag\n    ...\n}).\n```\n\n### Data Record (`#data{}` in ssh_connection_handler.erl)\n\n```erlang\n-record(data, {\n    socket,\n    transport_protocol,\n    ssh_params,             % #ssh{} record\n    connection_state,       % #connection{} record\n    ...\n}).\n```\n\n## Connection Message Types\n\nDefined in `ssh_connect.hrl`:\n\n```erlang\n#ssh_msg_global_request{}\n#ssh_msg_request_success{}\n#ssh_msg_request_failure{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_open{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_open_confirmation{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_open_failure{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_window_adjust{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_data{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_extended_data{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_eof{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_close{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_request{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_success{}\n#ssh_msg_channel_failure{}\n```\n\n## Authentication State Check Macro\n\n```erlang\n-define(CONNECTED(StateName),\n        (element(1,StateName) == connected orelse\n         element(1,StateName) == ext_info)).\n```\n\nThis macro checks if the connection is in an authenticated state.\n\n## Security-Critical Points\n\n1. **State-based guards**: Use `?CONNECTED(StateName)` to ensure operations only occur after authentication\n2. **Message validation**: Verify message types match expected state\n3. **Channel validation**: Check channel IDs and permissions\n4. **Authentication flag**: `authenticated = true` in `#ssh{}` record\n\n## Common Erlang Patterns\n\n### Guard Clauses\n```erlang\nhandle_event(cast, {request, ...}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    % Only process if connected (authenticated)\n```\n\n### Pattern Matching on Message Types\n```erlang\ncase Msg of\n    #ssh_msg_channel_open{} -> handle_open(Msg);\n    #ssh_msg_channel_data{} -> handle_data(Msg);\n    _ -> error\nend\n```\n\n## References\n\n- RFC 4251: SSH Protocol Architecture\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol\n- RFC 4253: SSH Transport Layer Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":439,"cache_read_input_tokens":28760,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":439,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1cd3d750-275e-4e32-b718-5590fb999419","uuid":"c39e30a2-1043-4d17-a190-84f2ec3f22b5"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_0144Tee4qEAhnPFrjLGkbonK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_017rz6taj5YwJ6M5zXdWFrRR","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/environment/skills/ssh-authentication-bypass-vulnerability.md","content":"# SSH Authentication Bypass Vulnerability Analysis\n\n## Overview\n\nThis document describes a class of SSH server vulnerabilities where connection protocol messages are processed before user authentication is complete, allowing unauthenticated remote code execution.\n\n## SSH Protocol State Machine\n\nAccording to RFC 4252/4254, the SSH protocol follows a strict sequence:\n\n1. **Transport Layer** - Version exchange, key exchange\n2. **Authentication** - User authentication (password, publickey, etc.)\n3. **Connection Protocol** - Channels, requests, data transfer\n\n**Critical Security Requirement**: Connection protocol messages (SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_*) MUST only be processed AFTER successful authentication.\n\n## Vulnerability Pattern\n\n### The Problem\n\nIn vulnerable implementations, the message dispatch logic processes connection messages based on their type, not the current connection state:\n\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE: No state check on connection messages\ncase decode(DecryptedBytes) of\n    #ssh_msg_channel_open{} = Msg -> process_channel_open(Msg);\n    #ssh_msg_channel_request{} = Msg -> process_channel_request(Msg);\n    ...\nend\n```\n\n### Attack Vector\n\nAn attacker can:\n1. Initiate SSH connection\n2. Complete key exchange (transport layer)\n3. Skip authentication entirely\n4. Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (open a channel)\n5. Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` with type \"exec\" and command payload\n6. Execute arbitrary commands as the SSH server user\n\n### Message Types That Enable Attack\n\n| Message | RFC | Purpose | Attack Use |\n|---------|-----|---------|------------|\n| `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (90) | 4254 | Open a channel | Create execution channel |\n| `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (98) | 4254 | Request action on channel | Execute \"exec\" request |\n| `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` (94) | 4254 | Send data to channel | Supply input to commands |\n\n### Specific Attack Payloads\n\nFor an \"exec\" request on a session channel:\n```\nSSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (98)\n├── recipient_channel: uint32 (target channel)\n├── request_type: string \"exec\"\n├── want_reply: boolean\n└── command: string (e.g., \"id\", \"cat /etc/passwd\", reverse shell, etc.)\n```\n\n## Detection\n\nLook for these code patterns:\n\n1. **Missing state guards** on connection message handlers\n2. **Unconditional dispatch** of decoded messages\n3. **No `authenticated` flag check** before processing channel operations\n\n### Example Vulnerable Pattern\n\n```erlang\n% Message decoded and dispatched without checking StateName\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, Msg}, StateName, D) ->\n    ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, ...).  % No guard!\n```\n\n### Example Secure Pattern\n\n```erlang\n% State check prevents processing before authentication\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, Msg}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, ...);  % Only if authenticated\n\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, _Msg}, StateName, D) ->\n    % Not connected/authenticated - disconnect\n    disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, \"Unexpected message\", StateName, D).\n```\n\n## Fix Strategy\n\n1. **Add state guard**: Ensure connection message handler has `?CONNECTED(StateName)` guard\n2. **Add fallback handler**: Process unexpected messages with disconnect/error\n3. **Validate at message decode**: Consider rejecting connection messages entirely if not in connected state\n\n## Impact\n\n- **CVSS Score**: 10.0 (Critical)\n- **Attack Complexity**: Low (no authentication required)\n- **Privileges Required**: None\n- **Impact**: Complete system compromise\n\n## Prevention\n\n1. State machine must enforce protocol sequence\n2. All channel operations must verify authentication state\n3. Defense in depth: validate at multiple layers (decode, dispatch, handler)\n\n## Related CVEs\n\n- CVE-2025-32433 (Erlang/OTP SSH)\n- Similar patterns exist in other SSH implementations\n\n## References\n\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1525,"cache_read_input_tokens":29199,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1525,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1cd3d750-275e-4e32-b718-5590fb999419","uuid":"5467791e-f7e3-45e6-9053-01420eee597e"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_017rz6taj5YwJ6M5zXdWFrRR","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /app/workspace/environment/skills/ssh-authentication-bypass-vulnerability.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1cd3d750-275e-4e32-b718-5590fb999419","uuid":"716d78b0-a478-4598-95c1-ace644db979a","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/app/workspace/environment/skills/ssh-authentication-bypass-vulnerability.md","content":"# SSH Authentication Bypass Vulnerability Analysis\n\n## Overview\n\nThis document describes a class of SSH server vulnerabilities where connection protocol messages are processed before user authentication is complete, allowing unauthenticated remote code execution.\n\n## SSH Protocol State Machine\n\nAccording to RFC 4252/4254, the SSH protocol follows a strict sequence:\n\n1. **Transport Layer** - Version exchange, key exchange\n2. **Authentication** - User authentication (password, publickey, etc.)\n3. **Connection Protocol** - Channels, requests, data transfer\n\n**Critical Security Requirement**: Connection protocol messages (SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_*) MUST only be processed AFTER successful authentication.\n\n## Vulnerability Pattern\n\n### The Problem\n\nIn vulnerable implementations, the message dispatch logic processes connection messages based on their type, not the current connection state:\n\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE: No state check on connection messages\ncase decode(DecryptedBytes) of\n    #ssh_msg_channel_open{} = Msg -> process_channel_open(Msg);\n    #ssh_msg_channel_request{} = Msg -> process_channel_request(Msg);\n    ...\nend\n```\n\n### Attack Vector\n\nAn attacker can:\n1. Initiate SSH connection\n2. Complete key exchange (transport layer)\n3. Skip authentication entirely\n4. Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (open a channel)\n5. Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` with type \"exec\" and command payload\n6. Execute arbitrary commands as the SSH server user\n\n### Message Types That Enable Attack\n\n| Message | RFC | Purpose | Attack Use |\n|---------|-----|---------|------------|\n| `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (90) | 4254 | Open a channel | Create execution channel |\n| `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (98) | 4254 | Request action on channel | Execute \"exec\" request |\n| `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` (94) | 4254 | Send data to channel | Supply input to commands |\n\n### Specific Attack Payloads\n\nFor an \"exec\" request on a session channel:\n```\nSSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (98)\n├── recipient_channel: uint32 (target channel)\n├── request_type: string \"exec\"\n├── want_reply: boolean\n└── command: string (e.g., \"id\", \"cat /etc/passwd\", reverse shell, etc.)\n```\n\n## Detection\n\nLook for these code patterns:\n\n1. **Missing state guards** on connection message handlers\n2. **Unconditional dispatch** of decoded messages\n3. **No `authenticated` flag check** before processing channel operations\n\n### Example Vulnerable Pattern\n\n```erlang\n% Message decoded and dispatched without checking StateName\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, Msg}, StateName, D) ->\n    ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, ...).  % No guard!\n```\n\n### Example Secure Pattern\n\n```erlang\n% State check prevents processing before authentication\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, Msg}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, ...);  % Only if authenticated\n\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, _Msg}, StateName, D) ->\n    % Not connected/authenticated - disconnect\n    disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, \"Unexpected message\", StateName, D).\n```\n\n## Fix Strategy\n\n1. **Add state guard**: Ensure connection message handler has `?CONNECTED(StateName)` guard\n2. **Add fallback handler**: Process unexpected messages with disconnect/error\n3. **Validate at message decode**: Consider rejecting connection messages entirely if not in connected state\n\n## Impact\n\n- **CVSS Score**: 10.0 (Critical)\n- **Attack Complexity**: Low (no authentication required)\n- **Privileges Required**: None\n- **Impact**: Complete system compromise\n\n## Prevention\n\n1. State machine must enforce protocol sequence\n2. All channel operations must verify authentication state\n3. Defense in depth: validate at multiple layers (decode, dispatch, handler)\n\n## Related CVEs\n\n- CVE-2025-32433 (Erlang/OTP SSH)\n- Similar patterns exist in other SSH implementations\n\n## References\n\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01Vzvy1VYqtEbv1UqHJiHqXV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01C1w17NYZYR3Gfjrv8hCrny","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/environment/skills/erlang-gen-statem-patterns.md","content":"# Erlang gen_statem Patterns for Security\n\n## Overview\n\n`gen_statem` is Erlang's behavior for implementing state machines. It's commonly used for protocol implementations like SSH, TLS, etc., where state-based security is critical.\n\n## Basic Structure\n\n```erlang\n-module(my_fsm).\n-behaviour(gen_statem).\n\n-export([callback_mode/0, init/1, handle_event/4]).\n\ncallback_mode() -> [handle_event_function, state_enter].\n\ninit(_Args) ->\n    {ok, initial_state, #data{}}.\n\nhandle_event(EventType, EventContent, State, Data) ->\n    % Handle events based on state\n    {next_state, NewState, NewData}.\n```\n\n## Event Types\n\n- `internal` - Internal events (often from `{next_event, internal, Msg}`)\n- `cast` - Asynchronous messages (gen_statem:cast)\n- `{call, From}` - Synchronous calls (gen_statem:call)\n- `info` - External messages (e.g., socket data)\n- `enter` - State entry events\n- `{timeout, Name}` - Named timeouts\n\n## State Guards for Security\n\n### Pattern: Restrict Operations to Authenticated State\n\n```erlang\n-define(AUTHENTICATED(State), element(1, State) == authenticated).\n\n% Only process sensitive operations when authenticated\nhandle_event({call, From}, {sensitive_operation, Args}, State, Data)\n  when ?AUTHENTICATED(State) ->\n    Result = perform_operation(Args, Data),\n    {keep_state, Data, [{reply, From, Result}]};\n\n% Reject when not authenticated\nhandle_event({call, From}, {sensitive_operation, _}, State, _Data) ->\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply, From, {error, not_authenticated}}]}.\n```\n\n### Pattern: Catch-All Handler for Unexpected Messages\n\n```erlang\n% IMPORTANT: Add fallback handlers for security\n% After specific handlers, add catch-all:\nhandle_event(internal, {connection_msg, Msg}, State, Data) when ?CONNECTED(State) ->\n    process_message(Msg, Data);\n\n% Fallback: disconnect on unexpected connection messages\nhandle_event(internal, {connection_msg, _Msg}, State, Data) ->\n    disconnect_with_error(\"Protocol error: unexpected message\", State, Data).\n```\n\n## Common Patterns\n\n### Next Event Generation\n\n```erlang\n% Generate internal event for later processing\n{keep_state, NewData, [{next_event, internal, {process, Item}}]}\n\n% Multiple events\n{keep_state, NewData, [\n    {next_event, internal, first_event},\n    {next_event, internal, second_event}\n]}\n```\n\n### State Transitions\n\n```erlang\n% Transition to new state\n{next_state, new_state, NewData}\n\n% Stay in current state\n{keep_state, NewData}\n{keep_state_and_data}\n\n% Stop the state machine\n{stop, Reason, Data}\n{stop_and_reply, Reason, Replies, Data}\n```\n\n### Replies\n\n```erlang\n% Reply to a call\n{keep_state, Data, [{reply, From, Result}]}\n\n% Multiple actions\n{keep_state, Data, [\n    {reply, From, ok},\n    {next_event, internal, continue}\n]}\n```\n\n## Security Anti-Patterns\n\n### AVOID: Processing Messages Without State Check\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: No state guard - processes in any state\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, Msg}, _State, Data) ->\n    process(Msg, Data).  % Security vulnerability!\n```\n\n### PREFER: State-Guarded Message Processing\n\n```erlang\n% GOOD: Only process when in appropriate state\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, Msg}, State, Data) when ?CONNECTED(State) ->\n    process(Msg, Data);\n\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg, _}, State, Data) ->\n    % Reject in other states\n    disconnect(\"Unexpected message\", State, Data).\n```\n\n## Pattern Matching in Handlers\n\n```erlang\n% Match specific message types\nhandle_event(internal, #auth_success{user = User}, {authenticating, _}, Data) ->\n    {next_state, {connected, server}, Data#data{user = User}};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #auth_failure{}, {authenticating, _}, Data) ->\n    try_next_auth_method(Data);\n\n% Pattern with guards\nhandle_event(info, {tcp, Sock, Bytes}, State, #data{socket = Sock} = Data)\n  when State =/= closed ->\n    process_incoming(Bytes, Data).\n```\n\n## State Representation\n\n### Tuple States (Common in SSH)\n\n```erlang\n% State is a tuple like {phase, role}\n{hello, server}\n{kexinit, client}\n{userauth, server}\n{connected, client}\n\n% Check with pattern matching or guards\n-define(CONNECTED(S), element(1, S) == connected).\n```\n\n### Atom States (Simpler Protocols)\n\n```erlang\ndisconnected\nconnecting\nauthenticating\nconnected\n```\n\n## Defensive Coding\n\n1. **Always have fallback handlers** for each event type\n2. **Use guards** to restrict sensitive operations to appropriate states\n3. **Log unexpected messages** for debugging and security monitoring\n4. **Validate state transitions** - don't allow skipping required phases\n\n```erlang\n% Complete handler coverage\nhandle_event(cast, _, State, _) when not ?CONNECTED(State) ->\n    {keep_state_and_data, [postpone]};  % Postpone until connected\n\nhandle_event(cast, Msg, State, Data) when ?CONNECTED(State) ->\n    process_cast(Msg, State, Data);\n\nhandle_event(_, _, _, _) ->\n    % Log and ignore unknown events\n    keep_state_and_data.\n```\n\n## References\n\n- Erlang gen_statem documentation\n- OTP Design Principles - State Machines\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1146,"cache_read_input_tokens":30724,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1146,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1cd3d750-275e-4e32-b718-5590fb999419","uuid":"3de82c05-f59b-4352-a580-ca49997526d5"}

connection_info(ConnectionHandler, conn_info_keys());\nconnection_info(ConnectionHandler, Key) when is_atom(Key) ->\n    case connection_info(ConnectionHandler, [Key]) of\n        [{Key,Val}] -> {Key,Val};\n        Other -> Other\n    end;\nconnection_info(ConnectionHandler, Options) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {connection_info, Options}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec channel_info(connection_ref(),\n\t\t   channel_id(),\n\t\t   [atom()]\n\t\t  ) -> proplists:proplist().\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nchannel_info(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId, Options) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {channel_info, ChannelId, Options}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec adjust_window(connection_ref(),\n\t\t    channel_id(),\n\t\t    integer()\n\t\t   ) -> ok.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nadjust_window(ConnectionHandler, Channel, Bytes) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {adjust_window, Channel, Bytes}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec close(connection_ref(),\n\t    channel_id()\n\t   ) -> ok.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nclose(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId) ->\n    case call(ConnectionHandler, {close, ChannelId}) of\n\tok ->\n\t    ok;\n\t{error, closed} ->\n\t    ok\n    end.\n\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nstore(ConnectionHandler, Key, Value) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {store,Key,Value}).\n\nretrieve(#connection{options=Opts}, Key) ->\n    try ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(Key, Opts) of\n        Value ->\n            {ok,Value}\n    catch\n        error:{badkey,Key} ->\n            undefined\n    end;\nretrieve(ConnectionHandler, Key) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {retrieve,Key}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nset_sock_opts(ConnectionRef, SocketOptions) ->\n    try lists:foldr(fun({Name,_Val}, Acc) ->\n                            case prohibited_sock_option(Name) of\n                                true -> [Name|Acc];\n                                false -> Acc\n                            end\n                    end, [], SocketOptions)\n    of\n        [] ->\n            call(ConnectionRef, {set_sock_opts,SocketOptions});\n        Bad ->\n            {error, {not_allowed,Bad}}\n    catch\n        _:_ ->\n            {error, badarg}\n    end.\n\nprohibited_sock_option(active)    -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(deliver)   -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(mode)      -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(packet)    -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(_) -> false.\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nget_sock_opts(ConnectionRef, SocketGetOptions) ->\n    call(ConnectionRef, {get_sock_opts,SocketGetOptions}).\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% Test support\n%%====================================================================\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec renegotiate(connection_ref()\n\t\t ) -> ok.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nrenegotiate(ConnectionHandler) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, force_renegotiate).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nalg(ConnectionHandler) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, get_alg).\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% Intitialisation\n%%====================================================================\ninit([Role, Socket, Opts]) when Role==client ; Role==server ->\n    process_flag(trap_exit, true),\n    %% ssh_params will be updated after receiving socket_control event\n    %% in wait_for_socket state;\n    D = #data{socket = Socket, ssh_params = #ssh{role = Role, opts = Opts}},\n    {ok, {wait_for_socket, Role}, D}.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Connection start and initialization helpers\ninit_connection_record(Role, Socket, Opts) ->\n    {WinSz, PktSz} = init_inet_buffers_window(Socket),\n    C = #connection{channel_cache = ssh_client_channel:cache_create(),\n                    channel_id_seed = 0,\n                    suggest_window_size = WinSz,\n                    suggest_packet_size = PktSz,\n                    requests = [],\n                    options = Opts,\n                    connection_supervisor = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(connection_sup, Opts)\n                   },\n    case Role of\n        server ->\n            C#connection{cli_spec =\n                             ?GET_OPT(ssh_cli, Opts, {ssh_cli,[?GET_OPT(shell, Opts)]}),\n                         exec =\n                             ?GET_OPT(exec, Opts)};\n        client ->\n            C\n    end.\n\ninit_ssh_record(Role, Socket, Opts) ->\n    %% Export of this internal function is\n    %% intended for low-level protocol test suites\n    {ok,PeerAddr} = inet:peername(Socket),\n    init_ssh_record(Role, Socket, PeerAddr, Opts).\n\ninit_ssh_record(Role, Socket, PeerAddr, Opts) ->\n    AuthMethods = ?GET_OPT(auth_methods, Opts),\n    S0 = #ssh{role = Role,\n\t      opts = Opts,\n\t      userauth_supported_methods = AuthMethods,\n\t      available_host_keys = available_hkey_algorithms(Role, Opts),\n\t      random_length_padding = ?GET_OPT(max_random_length_padding, Opts)\n\t   },\n\n    {Vsn, Version} = ssh_transport:versions(Role, Opts),\n    LocalName = case inet:sockname(Socket) of\n                    {ok,Local} -> Local;\n                    _ -> undefined\n                end,\n    case Role of\n\tclient ->\n\t    PeerName = case ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(host, Opts, element(1,PeerAddr)) of\n                           PeerIP when is_tuple(PeerIP) ->\n                               inet_parse:ntoa(PeerIP);\n                           PeerName0 when is_atom(PeerName0) ->\n                               atom_to_list(PeerName0);\n                           PeerName0 when is_list(PeerName0) ->\n                               PeerName0\n                       end,\n            ssh_lib:set_label(client,\n                              {connection, list_to_binary(PeerName), Socket}),\n            S1 =\n                S0#ssh{c_vsn = Vsn,\n                       c_version = Version,\n                       opts = ?PUT_INTERNAL_OPT({io_cb, case ?GET_OPT(user_interaction, Opts) of\n                                                            true ->  ssh_io;\n                                                            false -> ssh_no_io\n                                                        end},\n                                                Opts),\n                       userauth_quiet_mode = ?GET_OPT(quiet_mode, Opts),\n                       peer = {PeerName, PeerAddr},\n                       local = LocalName\n                      },\n            S1#ssh{userauth_pubkeys = [K || K <- ?GET_OPT(pref_public_key_algs, Opts),\n                                            is_usable_user_pubkey(K, S1)\n                                      ]\n                  };\n\n\tserver ->\n            ssh_lib:set_label(server, {connection, Socket}),\n\t    S0#ssh{s_vsn = Vsn,\n\t\t   s_version = Version,\n\t\t   userauth_methods = string:tokens(AuthMethods, \",\"),\n\t\t   kb_tries_left = 3,\n\t\t   peer = {undefined, PeerAddr},\n                   local = LocalName\n\t\t  }\n    end.\n\nhandshake(ConnPid, server, Ref, Timeout) ->\n    receive\n\t{ConnPid, ssh_connected} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {ok, ConnPid};\n\t{ConnPid, {not_connected, Reason}} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, {shutdown, Reason}} ->\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, Reason} ->\n\t    {error, Reason};\n        {'EXIT',_,Reason} ->\n            stop(ConnPid),\n            {error, {exit,Reason}}\n    after Timeout ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    ssh_connection_handler:stop(ConnPid),\n\t    {error, timeout}\n    end;\nhandshake(ConnPid, client, Ref, Timeout) ->\n    receive\n\t{ConnPid, ssh_connected} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {ok, ConnPid};\n\t{ConnPid, {not_connected, Reason}} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, {shutdown, Reason}} ->\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, Reason} ->\n\t    {error, Reason}\n    after Timeout ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    ssh_connection_handler:stop(ConnPid),\n\t    {error, timeout}\n    end.\n\nhandshake(Msg, #data{starter = User}) ->\n    User ! {self(), Msg}.\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% gen_statem callbacks\n%%====================================================================\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-type event_content() ::  any().\n\n-type renegotiate_flag() :: init | renegotiate.\n\n-type state_name() :: \n        {hello,                     role()                    }\n      | {kexinit,                   role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {key_exchange,              role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {key_exchange_dh_gex_init,  server, renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {key_exchange_dh_gex_reply, client, renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {new_keys,                  role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {ext_info,                  role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {service_request,           role()                    }\n      | {userauth,                  role()                    }\n      | {userauth_keyboard_interactive,       role()          }\n      | {userauth_keyboard_interactive_extra, server          }\n      | {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response, client  }\n      | {connected,                 role()                    }\n\t.\n\n%% The state names must fulfill some rules regarding\n%% where the role() and the renegotiate_flag() is placed:\n\n-spec renegotiation(state_name()) -> boolean().\nrenegotiation({_,_,ReNeg}) -> ReNeg == renegotiate;\nrenegotiation(_) -> false.\n\n\n-define(CONNECTED(StateName),\n        (element(1,StateName) == connected orelse\n         element(1,StateName) == ext_info ) ).\n\n-spec handle_event(gen_statem:event_type(),\n\t\t   event_content(),\n\t\t   state_name(),\n\t\t   #data{}\n\t\t  ) -> gen_statem:event_handler_result(state_name()) .\n\n-define(CONNECTION_MSG(Msg),\n        [{next_event, internal, prepare_next_packet},\n         {next_event,internal,{conn_msg,Msg}}]).\n\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\ncallback_mode() ->\n    [handle_event_function,\n     state_enter].\n\n%%% ######## {hello, client|server} ####\n%% The very first event that is sent when ssh_connection_handler\n%% becomes owner process for Socket\nhandle_event(cast, socket_control, {wait_for_socket, Role},\n             #data{socket = Socket, ssh_params = #ssh{opts = Opts}}) ->\n    case inet:peername(Socket) of\n        {ok, PeerAddr} ->\n            try\n                {Protocol, Callback, CloseTag} = ?GET_OPT(transport, Opts),\n                D = #data{starter = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(user_pid, Opts),\n                          socket = Socket,\n                          transport_protocol = Protocol,\n                          transport_cb = Callback,\n                          transport_close_tag = CloseTag,\n                          ssh_params = init_ssh_record(Role, Socket, PeerAddr, Opts),\n                          connection_state = init_connection_record(Role, Socket, Opts)\n                         },\n                NextEvent = {next_event, internal, socket_ready},\n                {next_state, {hello,Role}, D, NextEvent}\n            catch\n                _:{error,Error} -> {stop, {error,Error}};\n                error:Error ->     {stop, {error,Error}}\n            end;\n\n        {error,Error} ->\n            {stop, {shutdown,Error}}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, socket_ready, {hello,_}=StateName, #data{ssh_params = Ssh0} = D) ->\n    VsnMsg = ssh_transport:hello_version_msg(string_version(Ssh0)),\n    send_bytes(VsnMsg, D),\n    case inet:getopts(Socket=D#data.socket, [recbuf]) of\n\t{ok, [{recbuf,Size}]} ->\n\t    %% Set the socket to the hello text line handling mode:\n\t    inet:setopts(Socket, [{packet, line},\n\t\t\t\t  {active, once},\n\t\t\t\t  % Expecting the version string which might\n\t\t\t\t  % be max ?MAX_PROTO_VERSION bytes:\n\t\t\t\t  {recbuf, ?MAX_PROTO_VERSION},\n\t\t\t\t  {nodelay,true}]),\n            Time = ?GET_OPT(hello_timeout, Ssh0#ssh.opts, infinity),\n\t    {keep_state, D#data{inet_initial_recbuf_size=Size}, [{state_timeout,Time,no_hello_received}] };\n\n\tOther ->\n            ?call_disconnectfun_and_log_cond(\"Option return\", \n                                             io_lib:format(\"Unexpected getopts return:~n  ~p\",[Other]),\n                                             StateName, D),\n\t    {stop, {shutdown,{unexpected_getopts_return, Other}}}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, {info_line,_Line}, {hello,client}, D) ->\n    %% The server may send info lines to the client before the version_exchange\n    %% RFC4253/4.2\n    inet:setopts(D#data.socket, [{active, once}]),\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\nhandle_event(internal, {info_line,Line}, {hello,server}=StateName, D) ->\n    %% But the client may NOT send them to the server. Openssh answers with cleartext,\n    %% and so do we\n    send_bytes(\"Protocol mismatch.\", D),\n    Msg = io_lib:format(\"Protocol mismatch in version exchange. Client sent info lines.~n~s\",\n                        [ssh_dbg:hex_dump(Line, 64)]),\n    ?call_disconnectfun_and_log_cond(\"Protocol mismatch.\", Msg, StateName, D),\n    {stop, {shutdown,\"Protocol mismatch in version exchange. Client sent info lines.\"}};\n\nhandle_event(internal, {version_exchange,Version}, {hello,Role}, D0) ->\n    {NumVsn, StrVsn} = ssh_transport:handle_hello_version(Version),\n    case handle_version(NumVsn, StrVsn, D0#data.ssh_params) of\n\t{ok, Ssh1} ->\n\t    %% Since the hello part is finished correctly, we set the\n\t    %% socket to the packet handling mode (including recbuf size):\n\t    inet:setopts(D0#data.socket, [{packet,0},\n\t\t\t\t\t {mode,binary},\n\t\t\t\t\t {active, once},\n\t\t\t\t\t {recbuf, D0#data.inet_initial_recbuf_size}]),\n\t    {KeyInitMsg, SshPacket, Ssh} = ssh_transport:key_exchange_init_msg(Ssh1),\n\t    send_bytes(SshPacket, D0),\n            D = D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh,\n                        key_exchange_init_msg = KeyInitMsg},\n\t    {next_state, {kexinit,Role,init}, D, {change_callback_module, ssh_fsm_kexinit}};\n\n\tnot_supported ->\n            {Shutdown, D} =\n                ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_VERSION_NOT_SUPPORTED,\n                                 io_lib:format(\"Offending version is ~p\",[string:chomp(Version)]),\n                                 {hello,Role},\n                                 D0),\n\t    {stop, Shutdown, D}\n    end;\n\n%%% timeout after tcp:connect but then nothing arrives\nhandle_event(state_timeout, no_hello_received, {hello,_Role}=StateName, D0 = #data{ssh_params = Ssh0}) ->\n    MsgFun =\n        fun (debug) ->\n                Time = ?GET_OPT(hello_timeout, Ssh0#ssh.opts),\n                lists:concat([\"No HELLO received within \",ssh_lib:format_time_ms(Time)]);\n            (_) ->\n                [\"No HELLO received within hello_timeout\"]\n        end,\n    {Shutdown, D} =\n        ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ?SELECT_MSG(MsgFun), StateName, D0),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D};\n\n\n%%% ######## {service_request, client|server} ####\n\nhandle_event(internal, Msg = #ssh_msg_service_request{name=ServiceName}, StateName = {service_request,server}, D0) ->\n    case ServiceName of\n\t\"ssh-userauth\" ->\n\t    Ssh0 = #ssh{session_id=SessionId} = D0#data.ssh_params,\n\t    {ok, {Reply, Ssh}} = ssh_auth:handle_userauth_request(Msg, SessionId, Ssh0),\n            D = send_msg(Reply, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh}),\n\t    {next_state, {userauth,server}, D, {change_callback_module,ssh_fsm_userauth_server}};\n\n\t_ ->\n            {Shutdown, D} =\n                ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE,\n                                 io_lib:format(\"Unknown service: ~p\",[ServiceName]),\n                                 StateName, D0),\n            {stop, Shutdown, D}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_service_accept{name = \"ssh-userauth\"}, {service_request,client},\n\t     #data{ssh_params = #ssh{service=\"ssh-userauth\"} = Ssh0} = D0) ->\n    {Msg, Ssh} = ssh_auth:init_userauth_request_msg(Ssh0),\n    D = send_msg(Msg, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh,\n                              auth_user = Ssh#ssh.user\n                             }),\n    {next_state, {userauth,client}, D, {change_callback_module,ssh_fsm_userauth_client}};\n\n\n%% Skip ext_info messages in connected state (for example from OpenSSH >= 7.7)\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ext_info{}, {connected,_Role}, D) ->\n    {keep_state, D};\n\nhandle_event(internal, {#ssh_msg_kexinit{},_}, {connected,Role}, D0) ->\n    {KeyInitMsg, SshPacket, Ssh} = ssh_transport:key_exchange_init_msg(D0#data.ssh_params),\n    D = D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh,\n\t\tkey_exchange_init_msg = KeyInitMsg},\n    send_bytes(SshPacket, D),\n    {next_state, {kexinit,Role,renegotiate}, D, [postpone, {change_callback_module,ssh_fsm_kexinit}]};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_disconnect{description=Desc} = Msg, StateName, D0) ->\n    {disconnect, _, RepliesCon} =\n\tssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, D0#data.connection_state, ?role(StateName), D0#data.ssh_params),\n    {Actions,D} = send_replies(RepliesCon, D0),\n    disconnect_fun(\"Received disconnect: \"++Desc, D),\n    {stop_and_reply, {shutdown,Desc}, Actions, D};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ignore{}, {_StateName, _Role, init},\n             #data{ssh_params = #ssh{kex_strict_negotiated = true,\n                                     send_sequence = SendSeq,\n                                     recv_sequence = RecvSeq}}) ->\n    ?DISCONNECT(?SSH_DISCONNECT_KEY_EXCHANGE_FAILED,\n                io_lib:format(\"strict KEX violation: unexpected SSH_MSG_IGNORE \"\n                              \"send_sequence = ~p  recv_sequence = ~p\",\n                              [SendSeq, RecvSeq])\n               );\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ignore{}, _StateName, _) ->\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_unimplemented{}, _StateName, _) ->\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\n%% Quick fix of failing test case (ssh_options_SUITE:ssh_msg_debug_fun_option_{client|server}/1)\nhandle_event(cast, #ssh_msg_debug{} = Msg, State, D) ->\n    handle_event(internal, Msg, State, D);\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_debug{} = Msg, _StateName, D) ->\n    debug_fun(Msg, D),\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg,Msg}, StateName, #data{connection_state = Connection0,\n                                                        event_queue = Qev0} = D0) ->\n    Role = ?role(StateName),\n    Rengotation = renegotiation(StateName),\n    try ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, Connection0, Role, D0#data.ssh_params) of\n\t{disconnect, Reason0, RepliesConn} ->\n            {Repls, D} = send_replies(RepliesConn, D0),\n            case {Reason0,Role} of\n                {{_, Reason}, client} when ((StateName =/= {connected,client})\n                                            and (not Rengotation)) ->\n                    handshake({not_connected,Reason}, D);\n                _ ->\n                    ok\n            end,\n            {stop_and_reply, {shutdown,normal}, Repls, D};\n\n\t{Replies, Connection} when is_list(Replies) ->\n\t    {Repls, D} = \n\t\tcase StateName of\n\t\t    {connected,_} ->\n\t\t\tsend_replies(Replies, D0#data{connection_state=Connection});\n\t\t    _ ->\n\t\t\t{ConnReplies, NonConnReplies} = lists:splitwith(fun not_connected_filter/1, Replies),\n\t\t\tsend_replies(NonConnReplies, D0#data{event_queue = Qev0 ++ ConnReplies})\n\t\tend,\n            case {Msg, StateName} of\n                {#ssh_msg_channel_close{}, {connected,_}} ->\n                    {keep_state, D, [cond_set_idle_timer(D)|Repls]};\n                {#ssh_msg_channel_success{}, _} ->\n                    update_inet_buffers(D#data.socket),\n                    {keep_state, D, Repls};\n                _ ->\n                    {keep_state, D, Repls}\n            end\n\n    catch\n\tClass:Error ->\n            {Repls, D1} = send_replies(ssh_connection:handle_stop(Connection0), D0),\n            {Shutdown, D} = ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_BY_APPLICATION,\n                                             io_lib:format(\"Internal error: ~p:~p\",[Class,Error]),\n                                             StateName, D1),\n            {stop_and_reply, Shutdown, Repls, D}\n    end;\n\n\nhandle_event(enter, OldState, {connected,_}=NewState, D) ->\n    %% Entering the state where re-negotiation is possible\n    init_renegotiate_timers(OldState, NewState, D);\n\nhandle_event(enter, OldState, {ext_info,_,renegotiate}=NewState, D) ->\n    %% Could be hanging in exit_info state if nothing else arrives\n    init_renegotiate_timers(OldState, NewState, D);\n\nhandle_event(enter, {connected,_}=OldState, NewState, D) ->\n    %% Exiting the state where re-negotiation is possible\n    pause_renegotiate_timers(OldState, NewState, D);\n\nhandle_event(cast, force_renegotiate, StateName, D) ->\n    handle_event({timeout,renegotiate}, undefined, StateName, D);\n\nhandle_event({timeout,renegotiate}, _, StateName, D0) ->\n    case StateName of\n        {connected,Role} ->\n            start_rekeying(Role, D0);\n        {ext_info,Role,renegotiate} ->\n            start_rekeying(Role, D0);\n        _ ->\n            %% Wrong state for starting a renegotiation, must be in re-negotiation\n            keep_state_and_data\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({timeout,check_data_size}, _, StateName, D0) ->\n    %% Rekey due to sent data limit reached? (Can't be in {ext_info,...} if data is sent)\n    case StateName of\n        {connected,Role} ->\n            check_data_rekeying(Role, D0);\n        _ ->\n            %% Wrong state for starting a renegotiation, must be in re-negotiation\n            keep_state_and_data\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, get_alg, _, D) ->\n    #ssh{algorithms=Algs} = D#data.ssh_params,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Algs}]};\n\nhandle_event(cast, _, StateName, _) when not ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state_and_data, [postpone]};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {adjust_window,ChannelId,Bytes}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n\t#channel{recv_window_size = WinSize,\n\t\t recv_window_pending = Pending,\n\t\t recv_packet_size = PktSize} = Channel\n\t  when (WinSize-Bytes) >= 2*PktSize ->\n\t    %% The peer can send at least two more *full* packet, no hurry.\n\t    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(cache(D),\n\t\t\t\t     Channel#channel{recv_window_pending = Pending + Bytes}),\n\t    keep_state_and_data;\n\n\t#channel{recv_window_size = WinSize,\n\t\t recv_window_pending = Pending,\n\t\t remote_id = Id} = Channel ->\n\t    %% Now we have to update the window - we can't receive so many more pkts\n\t    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(cache(D),\n\t\t\t\t     Channel#channel{recv_window_size =\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t WinSize + Bytes + Pending,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t     recv_window_pending = 0}),\n\t    Msg = ssh_connection:channel_adjust_window_msg(Id, Bytes + Pending),\n\t    {keep_state, send_msg(Msg,D)};\n\n\tundefined ->\n\t    keep_state_and_data\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(cast, {reply_request,Resp,ChannelId}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n        #channel{remote_id = RemoteId} when Resp== success ; Resp==failure ->\n            Msg =\n                case Resp of\n                    success -> ssh_connection:channel_success_msg(RemoteId);\n                    failure -> ssh_connection:channel_failure_msg(RemoteId)\n                end,\n            update_inet_buffers(D#data.socket),\n            {keep_state, send_msg(Msg,D)};\n\n        #channel{} ->\n            Details = io_lib:format(\"Unhandled reply in state ~p:~n~p\", [StateName,Resp]),\n            {_Shutdown, D1} =\n                ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, Details, StateName, D),\n            {keep_state, D1};\n\n\tundefined ->\n\t    keep_state_and_data\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(cast, {request,ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state,  handle_request(ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data, false, none, D)};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {request,ChannelId,Type,Data}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state,  handle_request(ChannelId, Type, Data, false, none, D)};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {unknown,Data}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    Msg = #ssh_msg_unimplemented{sequence = Data},\n    {keep_state, send_msg(Msg,D)};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {global_request, Type, Data}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state, send_msg(ssh_connection:request_global_msg(Type,false,Data), D)};\n\n\n%%% Previously handle_sync_event began here\nhandle_event({call,From}, get_print_info, StateName, D) ->\n    Reply =\n\ttry\n\t    {inet:sockname(D#data.socket),\n\t     inet:peername(D#data.socket)\n\t    }\n\tof\n\t    {{ok,Local}, {ok,Remote}} ->\n\t\t{{Local,Remote},io_lib:format(\"statename=~p\",[StateName])};\n\t    _ ->\n\t\t{{\"-\",0},\"-\"}\n\tcatch\n\t    _:_ ->\n\t\t{{\"?\",0},\"?\"}\n\tend,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Reply}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {connection_info, Options}, _, D) ->\n    Info = fold_keys(Options, fun conn_info/2, D),\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Info}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {channel_info,ChannelId,Options}, _, D) ->\n    case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n\t#channel{} = Channel ->\n\t    Info = fold_keys(Options, fun chann_info/2, Channel),\n\t    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Info}]};\n\tundefined ->\n\t    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,[]}]}\n    end;\n\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {info, all}, _, D) ->\n    Result = ssh_client_channel:cache_foldl(fun(Channel, Acc) ->\n\t\t\t\t\t     [Channel | Acc]\n\t\t\t\t     end,\n\t\t\t\t     [], cache(D)),\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply, From, {ok,Result}}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {info, ChannelPid}, _, D) ->\n    Result = ssh_client_channel:cache_foldl(\n\t       fun(Channel, Acc) when Channel#channel.user == ChannelPid ->\n\t\t       [Channel | Acc];\n\t\t  (_, Acc) ->\n\t\t       Acc\n\t       end, [], cache(D)),\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply, From, {ok,Result}}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {set_sock_opts,SocketOptions}, _StateName, D) ->\n    Result = try inet:setopts(D#data.socket, SocketOptions)\n             catch\n                 _:_ -> {error, badarg}\n             end,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Result}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {get_sock_opts,SocketGetOptions}, _StateName, D) ->\n    Result = try inet:getopts(D#data.socket, SocketGetOptions)\n             catch\n                 _:_ -> {error, badarg}\n             end,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Result}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, stop, _StateName, D0) ->\n    {Repls,D} = send_replies(ssh_connection:handle_stop(D0#data.connection_state), D0),\n    {stop_and_reply, normal, [{reply,From,ok}|Repls], D};\n\nhandle_event({call,_}, _, StateName, _) when not ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state_and_data, [postpone]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {request, ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout}, StateName, D0)\n  when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    case handle_request(ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data, true, From, D0) of\n        {error,Error} ->\n            {keep_state, D0, {reply,From,{error,Error}}};\n        D ->\n            %% Note reply to channel will happen later when reply is received from peer on the socket\n            start_channel_request_timer(ChannelId, From, Timeout),\n            {keep_state, D, cond_set_idle_timer(D)}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {request, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout}, StateName, D0)\n  when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    case handle_request(ChannelId, Type, Data, true, From, D0) of\n        {error,Error} ->\n            {keep_state, D0, {reply,From,{error,Error}}};\n        D ->\n            %% Note reply to channel will happen later when reply is received from peer on the socket\n            start_channel_request_timer(ChannelId, From, Timeout),\n            {keep_state, D, cond_set_idle_timer(D)}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {global_request, \"tcpip-forward\" = Type,\n                           {ListenHost,ListenPort,ConnectToHost,ConnectToPort},\n                           Timeout}, StateName, D0) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    Id = make_ref(),\n    Data =  <<?STRING(ListenHost), ?Euint32(ListenPort)>>,\n    Fun = fun({success, <<Port:32/unsigned-integer>>}, C) ->\n                  Key = {tcpip_forward,ListenHost,Port},\n                  Value = {ConnectToHost,ConnectToPort},\n                  C#connection{options = ?PUT_INTERNAL_OPT({Key,Value}, C#connection.options)};\n             ({success, <<>>}, C) ->\n                  Key = {tcpip_forward,ListenHost,ListenPort},\n                  Value = {ConnectToHost,ConnectToPort},\n                  C#connection{options = ?PUT_INTERNAL_OPT({Key,Value}, C#connection.options)};\n             (_, C) ->\n                  C\n          end,\n    D = send_msg(ssh_connection:request_global_msg(Type, true, Data),\n                 add_request(Fun, Id, From, D0)),\n    start_channel_request_timer(Id, From, Timeout),\n    {keep_state, D, cond_set_idle_timer(D)};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {global_request, Type, Data, Timeout}, StateName, D0) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    Id = make_ref(),\n    D = send_msg(ssh_connection:request_global_msg(Type, true, Data),\n                 add_request(true, Id, From, D0)),\n    start_channel_request_timer(Id, From, Timeout),\n    {keep_state, D, cond_set_idle_timer(D)};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {data, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout}, StateName, D0)\n  when start_channel_request_timer(ChannelId, From, Timeout), % FIXME: No message exchange so why?\n    {keep_state, D, Repls};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {eof, ChannelId}, StateName, D0)\n  when D0),\n\t    {keep_state, D, [{reply,From,ok}]};\n\t_ ->\n\t    {keep_state, D0, [{reply,From,{error,closed}}]}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, get_misc, StateName,\n             #data{connection_state = #connection{options = Opts}} = D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    ConnectionSup = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(connection_sup, Opts),\n    Reply = {ok, {ConnectionSup, ?role(StateName), Opts}},\n    {keep_state, D, [{reply,From,Reply}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From},\n\t     {open, ChannelPid, Type, InitialWindowSize, MaxPacketSize, Data, Timeout},\n\t     StateName,\n\t     D0 = #data{connection_state = C}) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    erlang:monitor(process, ChannelPid),\n    {ChannelId, D1} = new_channel_id(D0),\n    WinSz = case InitialWindowSize of\n                undefined -> C#connection.suggest_window_size;\n                _ -> InitialWindowSize\n            end,\n    PktSz = case MaxPacketSize of\n                undefined -> C#connection.suggest_packet_size;\n                _ -> MaxPacketSize\n            end,\n    D2 = send_msg(ssh_connection:channel_open_msg(Type, ChannelId, WinSz, PktSz, Data),\n\t\t  D1),\n    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(cache(D2),\n\t\t\t     #channel{type = Type,\n\t\t\t\t      sys = \"none\",\n\t\t\t\t      user = ChannelPid,\n\t\t\t\t      local_id = ChannelId,\n\t\t\t\t      recv_window_size = WinSz,\n\t\t\t\t      recv_packet_size = PktSz,\n\t\t\t\t      send_buf = queue:new()\n\t\t\t\t     }),\n    D = add_request(true, ChannelId, From, D2),\n    start_channel_request_timer(ChannelId, From, Timeout),\n    {keep_state, D, cond_set_idle_timer(D)};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {send_window, ChannelId}, StateName, D)\n  when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    Reply = case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n\t\t#channel{send_window_size = WinSize,\n\t\t\t send_packet_size = Packsize} ->\n\t\t    {ok, {WinSize, Packsize}};\n\t\tundefined ->\n\t\t    {error, einval}\n\t    end,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Reply}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {recv_window, ChannelId}, StateName, D)\n  when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    Reply = case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n\t\t#channel{recv_window_size = WinSize,\n\t\t\t recv_packet_size = Packsize} ->\n\t\t    {ok, {WinSize, Packsize}};\n\t\tundefined ->\n\t\t    {error, einval}\n\t    end,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Reply}]};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {close, ChannelId}, StateName, D0)\n  when    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(cache(D1), Channel#channel{sent_close = true}),\n\t    {keep_state, D1, [cond_set_idle_timer(D1), {reply,From,ok}]};\n\tundefined ->\n\t    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,ok}]}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(cast, {store,Key,Value}, _StateName, #data{connection_state=C0} = D) ->\n    C = C0#connection{options = ?PUT_INTERNAL_OPT({Key,Value}, C0#connection.options)},\n    {keep_state, D#data{connection_state = C}};\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, {retrieve,Key}, _StateName, #data{connection_state=C}) ->\n    case retrieve(C, Key) of\n        {ok,Value} ->\n            {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,{ok,Value}}]};\n        _ ->\n            {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,undefined}]}\n    end;\n\n%%===== Reception of encrypted bytes, decryption and framing\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, Info}, {hello,_}, #data{socket = Sock,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t transport_protocol = Proto}) ->\n    case Info of\n\t\"SSH-\" ++ _ ->\n\t    {keep_state_and_data, [{next_event, internal, {version_exchange,Info}}]};\n\t_ ->\n\t    {keep_state_and_data, [{next_event, internal, {info_line,Info}}]}\n    end;\n\n\nhandle_event(info, {Proto, Sock, NewData}, StateName,\n             D0 = #data{socket = Sock,\n                        transport_protocol = Proto,\n                        ssh_params = SshParams}) ->\n    try ssh_transport:handle_packet_part(\n\t  D0#data.decrypted_data_buffer,\n\t  <<(D0#data.encrypted_data_buffer)/binary, NewData/binary>>,\n          D0#data.aead_data,\n          D0#data.undecrypted_packet_length,\n\t  D0#data.ssh_params)\n    of\n\t{packet_decrypted, DecryptedBytes, EncryptedDataRest, Ssh1} ->\n\t    D1 = D0#data{ssh_params =\n\t\t\t    Ssh1#ssh{recv_sequence = ssh_transport:next_seqnum(Ssh1#ssh.recv_sequence)},\n\t\t\tdecrypted_data_buffer = <<>>,\n                        undecrypted_packet_length = undefined,\n                        aead_data = <<>>,\n\t\t\tencrypted_data_buffer = EncryptedDataRest},\n\t    try\n\t\tssh_message:decode(set_kex_overload_prefix(DecryptedBytes,D1))\n\t    of\n\t\t#ssh_msg_kexinit{} = Msg ->\n\t\t    {keep_state, D1, [{next_event, internal, prepare_next_packet},\n\t\t\t\t     {next_event, internal, {Msg,DecryptedBytes}}\n\t\t\t\t    ]};\n\n                #ssh_msg_global_request{}            = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_request_success{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_request_failure{}           = [{{timeout, max_initial_idle_time}, cancel} |\n                                                                ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)\n                                                               ]};\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open_confirmation{} = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n                #ssh_msg_channel_open_failure{}      = Msg -> {keep_state, D1,
